FILE: docker-compose.yml
================================================================================
services:
  # 1. Реляційна база даних PostgreSQL 18
  postgres_db:
    image: postgres:18
    container_name: mysurvey_postgres
    restart: always
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: surveydb
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: mysecretpassword
      TZ: UTC
      PGTZ: UTC
    ports:
      - "5433:5432"
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql
    networks:
      - survey-network

  # 2. Документоорієнтована база даних MongoDB 8.0
  mongodb:
    image: mongo:8.0
    container_name: mysurvey_mongo
    restart: always
    environment:
      MONGO_INITDB_ROOT_USERNAME: mongoadmin
      MONGO_INITDB_ROOT_PASSWORD: mongosecretpassword
    ports:
      - "27017:27017"
    volumes:
      - mongo_data:/data/db
    networks:
      - survey-network

  # 3. Мікросервіс SqlService (без container_name і ports для підтримки --scale)
  sql_service:
    build:
      context: ./src
      dockerfile: MySurveyApp.SqlService/Dockerfile
    restart: always
    expose:
      - "8080"
    environment:
      - ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Development
      - ConnectionStrings__Postgres=Host=postgres_db;Port=5432;Database=surveydb;Username=postgres;Password=mysecretpassword
      - InternalApiKey=YOUR_SUPER_SECRET_MICROSERVICE_KEY_12345
    depends_on:
      - postgres_db
    networks:
      - survey-network

  # 4. Мікросервіс NoSqlService (без container_name і ports для підтримки --scale)
  nosql-service:
    build:
      context: ./src
      dockerfile: MySurveyApp.NoSqlService/Dockerfile
    restart: always
    expose:
      - "5002"
    environment:
      - ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Development
      - ASPNETCORE_HTTP_PORTS=5002
      - MongoDbSettings__ConnectionString=mongodb://mongoadmin:mongosecretpassword@mongodb:27017/?authSource=admin
      - MongoDbSettings__DatabaseName=MySurveyAppAnalyticsDb
      - MongoDbSettings__ResponsesCollectionName=survey_responses
      - SqlServiceSettings__BaseUrl=http://sql_service:8080
      - InternalApiKey=YOUR_SUPER_SECRET_MICROSERVICE_KEY_12345
    depends_on:
      - mongodb
      - sql_service
    networks:
      - survey-network

  # 5. API Gateway / Load Balancer (єдина зовнішня точка входу на порту 5000)
  api-gateway:
    build:
      context: ./src
      dockerfile: MySurveyApp.ApiGateway/Dockerfile
    container_name: mysurveyapp-gateway
    restart: always
    ports:
      - "5000:8080"
    environment:
      - ASPNETCORE_URLS=http://+:8080
      - ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Development
    depends_on:
      - sql_service
      - nosql-service
    networks:
      - survey-network

networks:
  survey-network:
    driver: bridge

volumes:
  postgres_data:
  mongo_data:

FILE: frontend\README.md
================================================================================
# React + TypeScript + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some Oxlint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the Oxlint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend enabling type-aware lint rules by installing `oxlint-tsgolint` and editing `.oxlintrc.json`:

```json
{
  "$schema": "./node_modules/oxlint/configuration_schema.json",
  "plugins": ["react", "typescript", "oxc"],
  "options": {
    "typeAware": true
  },
  "rules": {
    "react/rules-of-hooks": "error",
    "react/only-export-components": ["warn", { "allowConstantExport": true }]
  }
}
```

See the [Oxlint rules documentation](https://oxc.rs/docs/guide/usage/linter/rules) for the full list of rules and categories.

import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar';
import { HomePage } from './pages/HomePage';
import { AuthPage } from './pages/AuthPage';
import { ProfilePage } from './pages/ProfilePage';
import { CreateSurveyPage } from './pages/CreateSurveyPage';
import { MySurveysPage } from './pages/MySurveyPage';
import { SurveysFilterPage } from './pages/SurveysFilterPage';
import { TakeSurveyPage } from './pages/TakeSurveyPage';
import { SurveyAnalysisPage } from './pages/SurveyAnalysisPage';
import { ModerationPage } from './pages/ModerationPage';

export default function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-app text-main flex flex-col">
        <Navbar />
        <main className="flex-1 max-w-6xl w-full mx-auto px-4 pt-6 pb-24 md:py-10">
          <Routes>
            <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />
            <Route path="/auth" element={<AuthPage />} />
            <Route path="/login" element={<AuthPage />} />
            <Route path="/register" element={<AuthPage />} />
            <Route path="/profile" element={<ProfilePage />} />
            <Route path="/surveys/create" element={<CreateSurveyPage />} />
            <Route path="/my-surveys" element={<MySurveysPage />} />
            <Route path="/surveys" element={<SurveysFilterPage />} />
            <Route path="/survey/play" element={<TakeSurveyPage />} />
            <Route path="/survey/:id" element={<TakeSurveyPage />} />
            <Route path="/surveys/:id/analysis" element={<SurveyAnalysisPage />} />
            <Route path="/moderation" element={<ModerationPage />} />
          </Routes>
        </main>
      </div>
    </BrowserRouter>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\api\apiClient.ts
================================================================================
import axios from 'axios';

export const DEVICE_ID_KEY = 'mysurvey_device_id';
export const ANON_USER_ID_KEY = 'mysurvey_anon_user_id';
export const ANON_REGISTERED_KEY = 'mysurvey_anon_registered';
export const JWT_TOKEN_KEY = 'mysurvey_jwt_token';

function fnv1aHash(str: string, seed: number): string {
  let h = seed >>> 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
  }
  return (h >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

/**
 * Генерує та зберігає постійний унікальний ключ пристрою (deviceKey), який не змінюється.
 */
export function getPersistentDeviceId(): string {
  const cached = localStorage.getItem(DEVICE_ID_KEY);
  if (cached) return cached;

  const signature = [
    navigator.userAgent,
    navigator.language,
    `${screen.width}x${screen.height}`,
    screen.colorDepth,
    Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone,
    navigator.hardwareConcurrency ?? '0',
    navigator.platform ?? '',
  ].join('|');

  const p1 = fnv1aHash(signature, 0x811c9dc5);
  const p2 = fnv1aHash(signature, 0x01000193);
  const p3 = fnv1aHash(signature, 0x1b873593);
  const p4 = fnv1aHash(signature, 0x85ebca6b);

  const deviceKey = `${p1}-${p2.slice(0, 4)}-4${p2.slice(5, 8)}-a${p3.slice(1, 4)}-${p3.slice(4, 8)}${p4}`;

  localStorage.setItem(DEVICE_ID_KEY, deviceKey);
  return deviceKey;
}

export const apiClient = axios.create({
  baseURL: '/api',
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

apiClient.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const jwt = localStorage.getItem(JWT_TOKEN_KEY);
  const deviceKey = getPersistentDeviceId();
  const anonUserId = localStorage.getItem(ANON_USER_ID_KEY);

  if (jwt) {
    config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${jwt}`;
  } else {
    delete config.headers.Authorization;
  }

  config.headers['X-Device-Key'] = deviceKey;
  if (anonUserId) {
    config.headers['X-Anonymous-Token'] = anonUserId;
  }

  return config;
});

/**
 * Перевіряє в localStorage, чи вже створено анонімного користувача на пристрої.
 * Якщо ні — надсилає POST /api/Users/anonymous із { deviceKey } та зберігає отриманий userId.
 */
export async function ensureAnonymousSession(): Promise<{
  deviceId: string;
  userId: string;
  isRegistered: boolean;
  fromCache: boolean;
  error?: string;
}> {
  const deviceKey = getPersistentDeviceId();
  const hasJwt = Boolean(localStorage.getItem(JWT_TOKEN_KEY));
  const alreadyRegistered = localStorage.getItem(ANON_REGISTERED_KEY) === 'true';
  const cachedUserId = localStorage.getItem(ANON_USER_ID_KEY) || '';

  if (hasJwt || (alreadyRegistered && cachedUserId)) {
    return {
      deviceId: deviceKey,
      userId: cachedUserId,
      isRegistered: true,
      fromCache: true,
    };
  }

  try {
    const res = await apiClient.post('/Users/anonymous', {
      deviceKey,
    });

    const returnedUserId = String(res.data?.userId || '');
    if (!returnedUserId) {
      throw new Error('Сервер не повернув userId');
    }

    localStorage.setItem(ANON_USER_ID_KEY, returnedUserId);
    localStorage.setItem(ANON_REGISTERED_KEY, 'true');

    return {
      deviceId: deviceKey,
      userId: returnedUserId,
      isRegistered: true,
      fromCache: false,
    };
  } catch (err: any) {
    const status = err.response?.status;
    const msg = err.response?.data?.title || err.response?.data?.message || err.message;
    return {
      deviceId: deviceKey,
      userId: '',
      isRegistered: false,
      fromCache: false,
      error: `Помилка ${status || 'мережі'}: ${msg}`,
    };
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\components\SearchableSelect.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown, X, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import type { DictOption } from '../types/auth';

interface SearchableSelectProps {
  options: DictOption[];
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  placeholder?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
  emptyText?: string;
}

export function SearchableSelect({
  options,
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder = 'Оберіть зі списку...',
  disabled = false,
  icon,
  emptyText = 'Нічого не знайдено',
}: SearchableSelectProps) {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [highlightedIndex, setHighlightedIndex] = useState(0);

  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const selectedOption = useMemo(
    () => options.find((opt) => String(opt.id) === String(value)) ?? null,
    [options, value]
  );

  // Фільтрація: спочатку варіанти, що починаються з введених літер, потім інші збіги
  const filteredOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return options;

    const startsWith: DictOption[] = [];
    const includes: DictOption[] = [];

    for (const opt of options) {
      const lower = opt.name.toLowerCase();
      if (lower.startsWith(q)) {
        startsWith.push(opt);
      } else if (lower.includes(q)) {
        includes.push(opt);
      }
    }

    return [...startsWith, ...includes];
  }, [options, searchQuery]);

  // Закриття при кліку поза компонентом
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setIsOpen(false);
        setSearchQuery('');
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (disabled && isOpen) {
      setIsOpen(false);
      setSearchQuery('');
    }
  }, [disabled, isOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHighlightedIndex(0);
  }, [searchQuery, isOpen]);

  const openDropdown = () => {
    if (disabled) return;
    setIsOpen(true);
    setSearchQuery('');
    setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 0);
  };

  const handleSelect = (option: DictOption) => {
    onChange(String(option.id));
    setIsOpen(false);
    setSearchQuery('');
  };

  const handleClear = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (disabled) return;
    onChange('');
    setSearchQuery('');
    setIsOpen(true);
    inputRef.current?.focus();
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (disabled) return;

    if (!isOpen && (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'Enter')) {
      e.preventDefault();
      openDropdown();
      return;
    }

    if (!isOpen) return;

    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
      e.preventDefault();
      setHighlightedIndex((prev) =>
        filteredOptions.length > 0 ? (prev + 1) % filteredOptions.length : 0
      );
    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
      e.preventDefault();
      setHighlightedIndex((prev) =>
        filteredOptions.length > 0
          ? (prev - 1 + filteredOptions.length) % filteredOptions.length
          : 0
      );
    } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (filteredOptions[highlightedIndex]) {
        handleSelect(filteredOptions[highlightedIndex]);
      }
    } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      e.preventDefault();
      setIsOpen(false);
      setSearchQuery('');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} className="relative">
      <div
        onClick={openDropdown}
        className={`relative flex items-center rounded-xl bg-app border transition ${
          isOpen ? 'border-accent' : 'border-line'
        } ${disabled ? 'opacity-60 cursor-not-allowed' : 'cursor-text'}`}
      >
        {icon && (
          <span className="text-muted absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none">
            {icon}
          </span>
        )}

        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          type="text"
          disabled={disabled}
          value={isOpen ? searchQuery : selectedOption?.name ?? ''}
          placeholder={isOpen && selectedOption ? selectedOption.name : placeholder}
          onFocus={() => {
            if (!disabled && !isOpen) {
              setIsOpen(true);
              setSearchQuery('');
            }
          }}
          onChange={(e) => {
            if (!isOpen) setIsOpen(true);
            setSearchQuery(e.target.value);
          }}
          onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
          autoComplete="off"
          className={`w-full py-3 pr-14 bg-transparent text-main placeholder:text-muted text-sm outline-none disabled:cursor-not-allowed ${
            icon ? 'pl-10' : 'pl-3.5'
          }`}
        />

        <div className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex items-center gap-1">
          {!disabled && (value || searchQuery) && (
            <button
              type="button"
              tabIndex={-1}
              onClick={handleClear}
              title="Очистити"
              className="p-1 rounded-md text-muted hover:text-main transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          )}
          <ChevronDown
            className={`w-4 h-4 text-muted pointer-events-none transition-transform duration-150 ${
              isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''
            }`}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {isOpen && !disabled && (
        <ul className="absolute z-50 mt-1.5 w-full max-h-56 overflow-y-auto rounded-xl bg-card border border-line shadow-xl py-1 text-sm">
          {filteredOptions.length === 0 ? (
            <li className="px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-muted text-center">{emptyText}</li>
          ) : (
            filteredOptions.map((opt, idx) => {
              const isSelected = String(opt.id) === String(value);
              const isHighlighted = idx === highlightedIndex;

              return (
                <li
                  key={opt.id}
                  onMouseEnter={() => setHighlightedIndex(idx)}
                  onMouseDown={(e) => {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    handleSelect(opt);
                  }}
                  className={`px-3.5 py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 cursor-pointer transition ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-accent/15 text-accent font-semibold'
                      : isHighlighted
                      ? 'bg-app text-main'
                      : 'text-main hover:bg-app'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span className="truncate">{opt.name}</span>
                  {isSelected && <Check className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-accent" />}
                </li>
              );
            })
          )}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\components\SurveyChart.tsx

import {
  PieChart,
  Pie,
  Cell,
  Tooltip,
  ResponsiveContainer,
} from 'recharts';
import type { OptionStat } from '../types/survey';

export const CHART_COLORS = [
  '#3B82F6', // Синій
  '#10B981', // Смарагдовий
  '#F59E0B', // Бурштиновий
  '#EC4899', // Малиновий
  '#8B5CF6', // Фіолетовий
  '#06B6D4', // Бірюзовий
  '#F97316', // Помаранчевий
  '#14B8A6', // Хвойний
];

interface SurveyChartProps {
  stats: OptionStat[];
  totalResponses: number;
}

export function SurveyChart({ stats, totalResponses }: SurveyChartProps) {
  const hasVotes = totalResponses > 0 && stats.some((s) => s.count > 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Кольорова діаграма з відсотками */}
      <div className="relative h-64 sm:h-72 w-full">
        {hasVotes ? (
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
            <PieChart>
              <Pie
                data={stats}
                dataKey="percentage"
                nameKey="label"
                cx="50%"
                cy="50%"
                innerRadius="48%"
                outerRadius="82%"
                paddingAngle={3}
                label={({ percent }) =>
                  percent && percent >= 0.05 ? `${Math.round(percent * 100)}%` : ''
                }
                labelLine={false}
              >
                {stats.map((_, index) => (
                  <Cell
                    key={`cell-${index}`}
                    fill={CHART_COLORS[index % CHART_COLORS.length]}
                    stroke="transparent"
                  />
                ))}
              </Pie>
              <Tooltip
                formatter={(value: any, name: any) => [`${Number(value).toFixed(1)}%`, name]}
                contentStyle={{
                  backgroundColor: 'var(--bg-card)',
                  borderColor: 'var(--border-line)',
                  borderRadius: '12px',
                  color: 'var(--text-main)',
                  fontSize: '13px',
                }}
                itemStyle={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}
              />
            </PieChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        ) : (
          <div className="h-full rounded-2xl bg-app border border-line flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-4">
            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-muted">
              Поки що недостатньо голосів для побудови діаграми
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Центральний лічильник усередині кільця діаграми */}
        {hasVotes && (
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
            <span className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-main">{totalResponses}</span>
            <span className="text-[11px] sm:text-xs text-muted">всього голосів</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Розшифровка під діаграмою: що кожен колір означає */}
      <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-2 border-t border-line">
        <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          Позначення кольорів та розподіл голосів
        </h3>

        <div className="space-y-2">
          {stats.map((item, index) => {
            const color = CHART_COLORS[index % CHART_COLORS.length];
            const pct = Number.isFinite(item.percentage) ? item.percentage : 0;

            return (
              <div
                key={`${item.label}-${index}`}
                className="bg-app border border-line rounded-xl p-3 sm:p-3.5 space-y-2"
              >
                <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                    <span
                      className="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                      style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                    />
                    <span className="text-sm sm:text-base font-medium text-main break-words">
                      {item.label}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0 text-right">
                    <span className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-main">
                      {pct.toFixed(1)}%
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted">({item.count})</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Кольорова смужка прогресу для наочності */}
                <div className="h-1.5 w-full rounded-full bg-card overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                    style={{
                      width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, pct))}%`,
                      backgroundColor: color,
                    }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend\src\components\ThemeSwitcher.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Palette } from 'lucide-react';

export type ThemeId = 'ocean' | 'blitz' | 'daylight';

const THEMES: { id: ThemeId; name: string; dotColor: string }[] = [
  { id: 'ocean', name: 'Океан', dotColor: '#2563EB' },
  { id: 'blitz', name: 'Бліц', dotColor: '#E91E63' },
  { id: 'daylight', name: 'День', dotColor: '#F8FAFC' },
];

const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'mysurvey_theme';

export function ThemeSwitcher() {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<ThemeId>(() => {
    return (localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY) as ThemeId) || 'ocean';
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, theme);
  }, [theme]);

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-app border border-line rounded-xl p-1">
      <Palette className="w-4 h-4 text-muted ml-1.5 hidden sm:block" />
      {THEMES.map((t) => {
        const active = theme === t.id;
        return (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTheme(t.id)}
            title={`Колірна схема: ${t.name}`}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition cursor-pointer ${
              active
                ? 'bg-accent text-accent-text shadow-sm'
                : 'text-muted hover:text-main'
            }`}
          >
            <span
              className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full border border-black/20 shrink-0"
              style={{ backgroundColor: t.dotColor }}
            />
            <span>{t.name}</span>
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useLocation, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  LogIn,
  UserPlus,
  Lock,
  User,
  Calendar,
  Briefcase,
  MapPin,
  Heart,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  apiClient,
  ensureAnonymousSession,
  getPersistentDeviceId,
  ANON_USER_ID_KEY,
  ANON_REGISTERED_KEY,
  JWT_TOKEN_KEY,
} from '../api/apiClient';
import type {
  AuthUserResponse,
  DictOption,
  LoginRequest,
  RegisterRequest,
} from '../types/auth';
import { SearchableSelect } from '../components/SearchableSelect';

const USER_PROFILE_STORAGE = 'mysurvey_user_profile';
const USER_REGION_STORAGE = 'mysurvey_user_region_id';

function mapRegions(rawList: any[]): DictOption[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(rawList)) return [];
  return rawList
    .map((item) => ({
      id: Number(item.regionId ?? item.id ?? 0),
      name: String(item.regionName ?? item.name ?? ''),
    }))
    .filter((item) => item.id > 0 && item.name.length > 0);
}

function mapHobbies(rawList: any[]): DictOption[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(rawList)) return [];
  return rawList
    .map((item) => ({
      id: Number(item.hobbyId ?? item.id ?? 0),
      name: String(item.hobbyName ?? item.name ?? ''),
    }))
    .filter((item) => item.id > 0 && item.name.length > 0);
}

function mapOccupationDomains(rawList: any[]): DictOption[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(rawList)) return [];
  return rawList
    .map((item) => ({
      id: Number(item.occupationDomainId ?? item.domainId ?? item.id ?? 0),
      name: String(item.occupationDomainName ?? item.domainName ?? item.name ?? ''),
    }))
    .filter((item) => item.id > 0 && item.name.length > 0);
}

function mapOccupations(rawList: any[]): DictOption[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(rawList)) return [];
  return rawList
    .map((item) => ({
      id: Number(item.occupationId ?? item.id ?? 0),
      name: String(item.occupationName ?? item.name ?? ''),
    }))
    .filter((item) => item.id > 0 && item.name.length > 0);
}

export function AuthPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();

  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'login' | 'register'>(() =>
    searchParams.get('mode') === 'login' || location.pathname === '/login'
      ? 'login'
      : 'register'
  );
  const [deviceKey, setDeviceKey] = useState<string>(() => getPersistentDeviceId());

  // Спільні поля
  const [nickname, setNickname] = useState<string>('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState<string>('');
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState<string>('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Поля реєстрації
  const [gender, setGender] = useState<string>('');
  const [birthYear, setBirthYear] = useState<string>('');
  const [occupationDomainId, setOccupationDomainId] = useState<string>('');
  const [occupationId, setOccupationId] = useState<string>('');
  const [regionId, setRegionId] = useState<string>('');
  const [hobbyId, setHobbyId] = useState<string>('');

  // Довідники
  const [regions, setRegions] = useState<DictOption[]>([]);
  const [hobbies, setHobbies] = useState<DictOption[]>([]);
  const [occupationDomains, setOccupationDomains] = useState<DictOption[]>([]);
  const [occupations, setOccupations] = useState<DictOption[]>([]);

  const [loadingDicts, setLoadingDicts] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [loadingOccupations, setLoadingOccupations] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>('');
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState<string>('');

  // Синхронізація активної вкладки з URL (?mode=login / ?mode=register)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (searchParams.get('mode') === 'login' || location.pathname === '/login') {
      setMode('login');
    } else {
      setMode('register');
    }
  }, [location.pathname, searchParams]);

  // 1. Ініціалізуємо анонімну сесію, фіксуємо deviceKey пристрою та завантажуємо базові довідники
  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    async function initForm() {
      setLoadingDicts(true);
      try {
        const session = await ensureAnonymousSession();
        const currentDeviceKey = session.deviceId || getPersistentDeviceId();

        const [regionsRes, hobbiesRes, domainsRes] = await Promise.all([
          apiClient.get('/Dictionaries/regions'),
          apiClient.get('/Dictionaries/hobbies'),
          apiClient.get('/Dictionaries/occupation-domains'),
        ]);

        if (!isMounted) return;

        setDeviceKey(currentDeviceKey);
        setRegions(mapRegions(regionsRes.data));
        setHobbies(mapHobbies(hobbiesRes.data));
        setOccupationDomains(mapOccupationDomains(domainsRes.data));
      } catch (err: any) {
        if (!isMounted) return;
        const msg = err.response?.data?.message || err.message;
        setError(`Не вдалося завантажити довідники: ${msg}`);
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) setLoadingDicts(false);
      }
    }

    initForm();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  // 2. Каскадне завантаження професій після вибору галузі роботи
  useEffect(() => {
    setOccupationId('');
    setOccupations([]);

    if (!occupationDomainId) return;

    let isMounted = true;
    setLoadingOccupations(true);

    apiClient
      .get(`/Dictionaries/occupation-domains/${occupationDomainId}/occupations`)
      .then((res) => {
        if (isMounted) {
          setOccupations(mapOccupations(res.data));
        }
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (isMounted) {
          const msg = err.response?.data?.message || err.message;
          setError(`Не вдалося завантажити список професій: ${msg}`);
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (isMounted) setLoadingOccupations(false);
      });

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [occupationDomainId]);

  const passwordsMatch =
    password.length > 0 &&
    confirmPassword.length > 0 &&
    password === confirmPassword;

  const isRegisterValid =
    deviceKey.trim().length > 0 &&
    nickname.trim().length > 0 &&
    password.length >= 4 &&
    passwordsMatch &&
    gender !== '' &&
    birthYear !== '' &&
    Number(birthYear) >= 1920 &&
    Number(birthYear) <= new Date().getFullYear() - 6 &&
    occupationDomainId !== '' &&
    occupationId !== '' &&
    regionId !== '' &&
    hobbyId !== '';

  const isLoginValid = nickname.trim().length > 0 && password.length > 0;

  const saveSessionAndRedirect = async (rawResponse: any, message: string) => {
    const jwt = rawResponse?.token || rawResponse?.accessToken || '';
    if (jwt) {
      localStorage.setItem(JWT_TOKEN_KEY, jwt);
    }

    let profileData: AuthUserResponse = rawResponse?.user ?? rawResponse;

    if (!profileData?.nickname && jwt) {
      try {
        const profileRes = await apiClient.get<AuthUserResponse>('/Users/profile');
        profileData = profileRes.data;
      } catch {
        // Профіль підвантажиться безпосередньо на сторінці /profile
      }
    }

    if (profileData?.userId) {
      localStorage.setItem(ANON_USER_ID_KEY, profileData.userId);
      localStorage.setItem(ANON_REGISTERED_KEY, 'true');
    }
    if (profileData?.regionId) {
      localStorage.setItem(USER_REGION_STORAGE, String(profileData.regionId));
    }
    if (profileData?.nickname || profileData?.userId) {
      localStorage.setItem(USER_PROFILE_STORAGE, JSON.stringify(profileData));
    }

    Object.keys(sessionStorage).forEach((key) => {
      if (key.startsWith('mysurvey_topic_exhausted_')) {
        sessionStorage.removeItem(key);
      }
    });

    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('auth-changed'));

    setSuccessMsg(message);
    setTimeout(() => {
      navigate('/profile', { replace: true });
    }, 600);
  };

  const handleRegisterSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setSuccessMsg('');

    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError('Паролі не збігаються. Перевірте правильність введення.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isRegisterValid) {
      setError('Будь ласка, заповніть усі обов’язкові поля форми.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);

    const trimmedNickname = nickname.trim();
    const payload: RegisterRequest = {
      deviceKey: deviceKey.trim(),
      nickname: trimmedNickname,
      password,
      gender: Number(gender),
      birthYear: Number(birthYear),
      occupationId: Number(occupationId),
      regionId: Number(regionId),
      hobbyId: Number(hobbyId),
    };

    try {
      const registerRes = await apiClient.post<any>('/Users/register', payload);
      let authPayload = registerRes.data;

      if (!authPayload?.token && !authPayload?.accessToken) {
        const loginRes = await apiClient.post<any>('/Users/login', {
          nickname: trimmedNickname,
          password,
        });
        authPayload = loginRes.data;
      }

      await saveSessionAndRedirect(
        authPayload,
        `Акаунт «${trimmedNickname}» створено! Переходимо до профілю...`
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      const status = err.response?.status;
      const msg =
        err.response?.data?.message ||
        err.response?.data?.title ||
        (typeof err.response?.data === 'string' ? err.response.data : err.message);
      setError(`Помилка реєстрації (${status || 'мережа'}): ${msg}`);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLoginSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError('');
    setSuccessMsg('');

    if (!isLoginValid) {
      setError('Введіть нікнейм та пароль.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);

    const trimmedNickname = nickname.trim();
    const payload: LoginRequest = {
      nickname: trimmedNickname,
      password,
    };

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.post<any>('/Users/login', payload);
      await saveSessionAndRedirect(
        res.data,
        `Вітаємо, ${res.data?.nickname || trimmedNickname}! Вхід виконано.`
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      const status = err.response?.status;
      const msg =
        err.response?.data?.message ||
        err.response?.data?.title ||
        (typeof err.response?.data === 'string' ? err.response.data : err.message);
      setError(`Помилка входу (${status || 'мережа'}): ${msg}`);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const switchMode = (nextMode: 'login' | 'register') => {
    setMode(nextMode);
    setSearchParams({ mode: nextMode }, { replace: true });
    setError('');
    setSuccessMsg('');
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-xl mx-auto space-y-5">
      <div className="bg-card border border-line rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-sm space-y-6">
        {/* Перемикач Вхід / Реєстрація */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-app border border-line">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => switchMode('login')}
            className={`py-2.5 px-4 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition cursor-pointer ${
              mode === 'login'
                ? 'bg-accent text-accent-text shadow-sm'
                : 'text-muted hover:text-main'
            }`}
          >
            <LogIn className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Вхід</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => switchMode('register')}
            className={`py-2.5 px-4 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition cursor-pointer ${
              mode === 'register'
                ? 'bg-accent text-accent-text shadow-sm'
                : 'text-muted hover:text-main'
            }`}
          >
            <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Реєстрація</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Заголовок */}
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-main">
            {mode === 'register' ? 'Створення профілю' : 'Вхід в акаунт'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-muted">
            {mode === 'register'
              ? 'Усі накопичені анонімні відповіді та токени питань прив’яжуться до вашого акаунта.'
              : 'Увійдіть за своїм нікнеймом та паролем.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Повідомлення про помилку / успіх */}
        {error && (
          <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/30 text-rose-500 text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium flex items-start gap-2.5">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {successMsg && (
          <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-500 text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
            <span>{successMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {mode === 'login' ? (
          /* ================= ФОРМА ВХОДУ ================= */
          <form onSubmit={handleLoginSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                Нікнейм <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <User className="w-4 h-4 text-muted absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={nickname}
                  onChange={(e) => setNickname(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Введіть нікнейм"
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-app border border-line text-main text-sm focus:border-accent outline-none transition"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                Пароль <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-muted absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  required
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Введіть пароль"
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-10 py-3 rounded-xl bg-app border border-line text-main text-sm focus:border-accent outline-none transition"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword((prev) => !prev)}
                  className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted hover:text-main cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={!isLoginValid || submitting}
              className="w-full py-3.5 px-6 rounded-xl bg-accent hover:bg-accent-hover disabled:opacity-50 text-accent-text font-semibold text-sm sm:text-base inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition shadow-sm cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
            >
              <LogIn className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{submitting ? 'Виконується вхід...' : 'Увійти'}</span>
            </button>
          </form>
        ) : (
          /* ================= ФОРМА РЕЄСТРАЦІЇ ================= */
          <form onSubmit={handleRegisterSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            {/* Нікнейм */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                Нікнейм <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <User className="w-4 h-4 text-muted absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={nickname}
                  onChange={(e) => setNickname(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Придумайте унікальний нікнейм"
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-app border border-line text-main text-sm focus:border-accent outline-none transition"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Пароль та Повтор пароля */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                  Пароль <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-muted absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    required
                    minLength={4}
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Мінімум 4 символи"
                    className="w-full pl-10 pr-9 py-3 rounded-xl bg-app border border-line text-main text-sm focus:border-accent outline-none transition"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPassword((prev) => !prev)}
                    className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted hover:text-main cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                  Повторити пароль <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-muted absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    required
                    value={confirmPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Повторіть пароль"
                    className={`w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-app border text-main text-sm outline-none transition ${
                      confirmPassword.length > 0
                        ? passwordsMatch
                          ? 'border-emerald-500'
                          : 'border-rose-500'
                        : 'border-line focus:border-accent'
                    }`}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {confirmPassword.length > 0 && !passwordsMatch && (
              <p className="text-xs text-rose-500 font-medium">Паролі не збігаються</p>
            )}

            {/* Стать та Рік народження */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                  Стать <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <select
                  required
                  value={gender}
                  onChange={(e) => setGender(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-3 rounded-xl bg-app border border-line text-main text-sm focus:border-accent outline-none transition"
                >
                  <option value="">Оберіть стать...</option>
                  <option value="0">Чоловіча</option>
                  <option value="1">Жіноча</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                  Рік народження <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-muted absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    min={1920}
                    max={new Date().getFullYear() - 6}
                    value={birthYear}
                    onChange={(e) => setBirthYear(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Наприклад, 2004"
                    className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-app border border-line text-main text-sm focus:border-accent outline-none transition"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Галузь роботи та Професія / спеціальність */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                  Галузь роботи <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <SearchableSelect
                  options={occupationDomains}
                  value={occupationDomainId}
                  onChange={setOccupationDomainId}
                  disabled={loadingDicts}
                  placeholder={loadingDicts ? 'Завантаження...' : 'Почніть вводити галузь...'}
                  icon={<Briefcase className="w-4 h-4" />}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                  Професія / спеціальність <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <SearchableSelect
                  options={occupations}
                  value={occupationId}
                  onChange={setOccupationId}
                  disabled={!occupationDomainId || loadingOccupations}
                  placeholder={
                    !occupationDomainId
                      ? 'Спочатку оберіть галузь'
                      : loadingOccupations
                      ? 'Завантаження...'
                      : 'Почніть вводити професію...'
                  }
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Регіон та Хобі */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                  Регіон <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <SearchableSelect
                  options={regions}
                  value={regionId}
                  onChange={setRegionId}
                  disabled={loadingDicts}
                  placeholder={loadingDicts ? 'Завантаження...' : 'Почніть вводити регіон...'}
                  icon={<MapPin className="w-4 h-4" />}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-muted">
                  Хобі <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <SearchableSelect
                  options={hobbies}
                  value={hobbyId}
                  onChange={setHobbyId}
                  disabled={loadingDicts}
                  placeholder={loadingDicts ? 'Завантаження...' : 'Почніть вводити хобі...'}
                  icon={<Heart className="w-4 h-4" />}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={!isRegisterValid || submitting || loadingDicts}
              className="w-full py-3.5 px-6 rounded-xl bg-accent hover:bg-accent-hover disabled:opacity-50 text-accent-text font-semibold text-sm sm:text-base inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition shadow-sm cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
            >
              <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{submitting ? 'Реєстрація...' : 'Зареєструватися'}</span>
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\CreateSurveyPage.tsx
