      const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SHUFFLE_BAG_KEY) || '[]');
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        bag = parsed.filter((v) => v === 0 || v === 1);
      }
    } catch {
      bag = [];
    }
  }

  if (bag.length === 0) {
    bag = buildShuffleBag(filters.preferredCount);
  }

  const randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * bag.length);
  const picked = (bag[randomIndex] === 1 ? 1 : 0) as 0 | 1;

  bag.splice(randomIndex, 1);
  localStorage.setItem(SHUFFLE_BAG_KEY, JSON.stringify(bag));
  localStorage.setItem(SHUFFLE_BAG_META_KEY, metaSignature);

  return picked;
}

export function TakeSurveyPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const hasLoadedRef = useRef(false);

  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<SavedSurveyFilters | null>(null);
  const [survey, setSurvey] = useState<RandomSurveyDto | null>(null);
  const [isPreferredPick, setIsPreferredPick] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [topicExhausted, setTopicExhausted] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const [selectedOptionIds, setSelectedOptionIds] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [openAnswer, setOpenAnswer] = useState<string>('');

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>('');
  const [emptyPoolMessage, setEmptyPoolMessage] = useState<string>('');

  const fetchNextQuestion = async (activeFilters: SavedSurveyFilters) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    setEmptyPoolMessage('');
    setSurvey(null);
    setSelectedOptionIds([]);
    setOpenAnswer('');

    const exhaustedKey = `${TOPIC_EXHAUSTED_PREFIX}${activeFilters.topicId}`;
    setTopicExhausted(sessionStorage.getItem(exhaustedKey) === 'true');

    try {
      await ensureAnonymousSession();

      const flag = drawNextTopicFlag(activeFilters);
      const preferred = flag === 1;
      setIsPreferredPick(preferred);

      const savedRegionId = localStorage.getItem('mysurvey_user_region_id');
      const regionParam = savedRegionId ? { regionId: Number(savedRegionId) } : {};

      const primaryParams = preferred
        ? { topicId: activeFilters.topicId, ...regionParam }
        : { excludeTopicId: activeFilters.topicId, ...regionParam };

      const fallbackParams = preferred
        ? { excludeTopicId: activeFilters.topicId, ...regionParam }
        : { topicId: activeFilters.topicId, ...regionParam };

      try {
        const res = await apiClient.get<RandomSurveyDto>('/Surveys/random', {
          params: primaryParams,
        });
        if (preferred) {
          sessionStorage.removeItem(exhaustedKey);
          setTopicExhausted(false);
        }
        setSurvey(res.data);
      } catch (firstErr: any) {
        if (firstErr.response?.status === 404) {
          if (preferred) {
            // Усі питання з вибраної теми закінчилися — вмикаємо банер і переходимо на рандомні теми
            sessionStorage.setItem(exhaustedKey, 'true');
            setTopicExhausted(true);
          }

          setIsPreferredPick(!preferred);
          const fallbackRes = await apiClient.get<RandomSurveyDto>('/Surveys/random', {
            params: fallbackParams,
          });

          if (!preferred) {
            sessionStorage.removeItem(exhaustedKey);
            setTopicExhausted(false);
          }
          setSurvey(fallbackRes.data);
        } else {
          throw firstErr;
        }
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.response?.status === 404) {
        setEmptyPoolMessage(
          err.response?.data?.message ||
            'Ви вже відповіли на всі доступні активні опитування для обраних параметрів.'
        );
      } else {
        const status = err.response?.status;
        const msg = err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data?.title || err.message;
        setError(`Не вдалося завантажити питання (${status || 'мережа'}): ${msg}`);
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasLoadedRef.current) return;
    hasLoadedRef.current = true;

    const rawFilters = localStorage.getItem(SAVED_FILTERS_KEY);
    if (!rawFilters) {
      navigate('/surveys', { replace: true });
      return;
    }

    try {
      const parsed: SavedSurveyFilters = JSON.parse(rawFilters);
      if (!parsed.topicId) {
        navigate('/surveys', { replace: true });
        return;
      }
      setFilters(parsed);
      fetchNextQuestion(parsed);
    } catch {
      navigate('/surveys', { replace: true });
    }
  }, [navigate]);

  const normalizedType = survey?.surveyType?.toLowerCase() ?? '';
  const options = survey?.options ?? [];
  const isMultiple = normalizedType.includes('multiple');
  const isRatingScale = normalizedType.includes('rating');
  const isNumericInput = isRatingScale || normalizedType.includes('numeric');
  const isOpenOrNumeric =
    options.length === 0 ||
    ['numeric', 'open', 'text'].some((t) => normalizedType.includes(t));

  const handleToggleOption = (optionId: number) => {
    if (isMultiple) {
      setSelectedOptionIds((prev) =>
        prev.includes(optionId) ? prev.filter((id) => id !== optionId) : [...prev, optionId]
      );
    } else {
      setSelectedOptionIds([optionId]);
    }
  };

  const canSubmit = isOpenOrNumeric
    ? openAnswer.trim().length > 0
    : selectedOptionIds.length > 0;

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!survey || !canSubmit || submitting) return;

    setSubmitting(true);
    setError('');

    const payload: SubmitResponseRequest = {
      surveyId: survey.surveyId,
      selectedOptionIds: isOpenOrNumeric ? [] : selectedOptionIds,
      openAnswer: isOpenOrNumeric ? openAnswer.trim() : null,
      isPreferredDomain: isPreferredPick,
    };

    try {
      await apiClient.post('/internal/responses', payload);
      navigate(`/surveys/${survey.surveyId}/analysis`, {
        state: {
          completed: true,
          surveyId: survey.surveyId,
          questionText: survey.surveyText,
        },
      });
    } catch (err: any) {
      const status = err.response?.status;
      if (status === 409) {
        navigate(`/surveys/${survey.surveyId}/analysis`, {
          state: {
            completed: true,
            surveyId: survey.surveyId,
            questionText: survey.surveyText,
          },
        });
        return;
      }
      const msg = err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data?.title || err.message;
      setError(`Помилка відправки відповіді (${status || 'мережа'}): ${msg}`);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto bg-card border border-line rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-10 space-y-5 animate-pulse">
        <div className="h-5 w-44 bg-app rounded-lg" />
        <div className="h-8 w-full bg-app rounded-xl" />
        <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
          <div className="h-14 bg-app rounded-xl" />
          <div className="h-14 bg-app rounded-xl" />
          <div className="h-14 bg-app rounded-xl" />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (emptyPoolMessage) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto bg-card border border-line rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-center space-y-5">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-app border border-line flex items-center justify-center text-highlight mx-auto">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-6 h-6" />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-main">Усі доступні опитування пройдено</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted">{emptyPoolMessage}</p>
        </div>
        <Link
          to="/surveys"
          className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-3 rounded-xl bg-accent hover:bg-accent-hover text-accent-text text-sm font-semibold transition"
        >
          <SlidersHorizontal className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>Змінити тему у фільтрах</span>
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error && !survey) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto bg-card border border-rose-800/70 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 text-rose-400">
          <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <p className="text-sm">{error}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          {filters && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => fetchNextQuestion(filters)}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-accent hover:bg-accent-hover text-accent-text text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              Спробувати ще раз
            </button>
          )}
          <Link
            to="/surveys"
            className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-app border border-line text-main text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium transition"
          >
            До фільтрів
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!survey) return null;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto space-y-4">
      {/* Верхня панель інформації про поточне питання та джерело (обрана тема / рандом) */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-card border border-line text-highlight font-medium">
          {isPreferredPick ? (
            <>
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Обрана тема: {filters?.topicName || `#${survey.topicId}`}</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Shuffle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Випадкова тема (мікс {filters?.preferredCount}/{filters?.randomCount})</span>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        <Link
          to="/surveys"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-card border border-line text-muted hover:text-main transition"
        >
          <SlidersHorizontal className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>Змінити фільтр</span>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* Банер-повідомлення, коли питання в обраній темі вичерпано */}
      {topicExhausted && (
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/40 text-xs sm:text-sm">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
            <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <p className="font-semibold text-main">
                Ви вже відповіли на всі питання з теми «{filters?.topicName || `#${filters?.topicId}`}»
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted">
                Продовжуємо показувати випадкові питання з інших тем.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Link
            to="/surveys"
            className="shrink-0 self-start sm:self-center px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-card border border-line hover:border-accent text-xs font-medium text-main transition"
          >
            Обрати іншу тему
          </Link>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Картка опитування */}
      <div className="bg-card border border-line rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-8 space-y-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <span className="text-xs text-muted">
            {isOpenOrNumeric
              ? isRatingScale
                ? 'Оцініть за шкалою (введіть число)'
                : 'Відкрита / числова відповідь'
              : isMultiple
              ? 'Оберіть один або кілька варіантів'
              : isRatingScale
              ? 'Оберіть оцінку за шкалою'
              : 'Оберіть один варіант відповіді'}
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-main leading-snug">
            {survey.surveyText}
          </h1>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-950/60 border border-rose-800 text-rose-300 text-xs sm:text-sm">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
          {isOpenOrNumeric ? (
            <div>
              <input
                type={isNumericInput ? 'number' : 'text'}
                step={isRatingScale ? '1' : 'any'}
                min={isRatingScale ? '1' : undefined}
                max={isRatingScale ? '10' : undefined}
                required
                placeholder={isRatingScale ? 'Введіть оцінку...' : 'Введіть вашу відповідь...'}
                value={openAnswer}
                onChange={(e) => setOpenAnswer(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-app border border-line text-main text-base focus:border-accent outline-none transition"
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {options.map((opt) => {
                const isSelected = selectedOptionIds.includes(opt.optionId);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={opt.optionId}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleToggleOption(opt.optionId)}
                    className={`w-full text-left px-4 py-3.5 rounded-xl border transition flex items-center justify-between gap-3 cursor-pointer ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-accent/15 border-accent text-main font-medium shadow-sm'
                        : 'bg-app border-line text-muted hover:text-main hover:border-accent/50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span className="text-sm sm:text-base">{opt.optionText}</span>
                    <span className={isSelected ? 'text-accent shrink-0' : 'text-muted shrink-0'}>
                      {isMultiple ? (
                        isSelected ? (
                          <CheckSquare className="w-5 h-5" />
                        ) : (
                          <Square className="w-5 h-5" />
                        )
                      ) : isSelected ? (
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5" />
                      ) : (
                        <Circle className="w-5 h-5" />
                      )}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={!canSubmit || submitting}
            className="w-full py-3.5 px-6 rounded-xl bg-accent hover:bg-accent-hover disabled:opacity-50 text-accent-text font-semibold text-base inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 transition shadow-sm cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{submitting ? 'Надсилання відповіді...' : 'Відповісти'}</span>
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\types\survey.ts
================================================================================
export type SurveyType = 'SingleChoice' | 'MultipleChoice' | 'Numeric';
export type SurveyStatus = 'Active' | 'Inactive' | 'Pending';

export interface SurveyOption {
  optionId: number;
  optionText: string;
  orderIndex: number;
}

export interface Survey {
  surveyId: number;
  questionText: string;
  surveyType: SurveyType;
  status: SurveyStatus;
  domainName: string;
  topicName: string;
  regionName: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  options: SurveyOption[];
}

export interface UserProfile {
  userId: string;
  email: string;
  userType: 'Anonymous' | 'User' | 'Moderator';
  answersCount: number;
  gender: string | null;
  birthYear: number | null;
  regionName: string | null;
  occDomainName: string | null;
  occupationName: string | null;
  hobbyName: string | null;
}

export interface OptionStat {
  label: string;
  count: number;
  percentage: number;
}

export interface SurveyDiagramData {
  surveyId: number;
  questionText: string;
  surveyType: SurveyType;
  totalResponses: number;
  averageAnsweredMs: number;
  stats: OptionStat[];
}

export interface SurveyDomain {
  domainId: number;
  domainName: string;
}

export interface SurveyTopic {
  topicId: number;
  domainId: number;
  topicName: string;
}

export type PreferredTopicCount = 5 | 6 | 7 | 8;

export interface SavedSurveyFilters {
  domainId: number;
  domainName: string;
  topicId: number;
  topicName: string;
  preferredCount: PreferredTopicCount; // Скільки питань з обраної теми (5..8)
  randomCount: number;                 // Скільки рандомних питань (5..2)
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface RandomSurveyOption {
  optionId: number;
  optionText: string;
}

export interface RandomSurveyDto {
  surveyId: number;
  surveyText: string;
  surveyType: string; // 'SingleChoice' | 'MultipleChoice' | 'Numeric' | 'Open'
  topicId: number;
  options: RandomSurveyOption[];
}

export interface SubmitResponseRequest {
  surveyId: number;
  selectedOptionIds: number[];
  openAnswer: string | null;
  isPreferredDomain: boolean;
}

FROM mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/aspnet:10.0 AS base
WORKDIR /app
EXPOSE 8080

FROM mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/sdk:10.0 AS build
WORKDIR /src
COPY ["MySurveyApp.ApiGateway/MySurveyApp.ApiGateway.csproj", "MySurveyApp.ApiGateway/"]
RUN dotnet restore "MySurveyApp.ApiGateway/MySurveyApp.ApiGateway.csproj"
COPY . .
WORKDIR "/src/MySurveyApp.ApiGateway"
RUN dotnet build "MySurveyApp.ApiGateway.csproj" -c Release -o /app/build

FROM build AS publish
RUN dotnet publish "MySurveyApp.ApiGateway.csproj" -c Release -o /app/publish /p:UseAppHost=false

FROM base AS final
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=publish /app/publish .
ENTRYPOINT ["dotnet", "MySurveyApp.ApiGateway.dll"]

FILE: src\MySurveyApp.ApiGateway\Program.cs

using Yarp.ReverseProxy.Transforms;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
{
    options.AddPolicy("FrontendPolicy", policy =>
    {
        policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
              .AllowAnyHeader()
              .AllowAnyMethod()
              .WithExposedHeaders("X-Handled-By");
    });
});

builder.Services.AddReverseProxy()
    .LoadFromConfig(builder.Configuration.GetSection("ReverseProxy"))
    .AddDnsDestinationResolver()
    .AddTransforms(context =>
    {
        context.AddResponseTransform(responseContext =>
        {
            var upstream = responseContext.ProxyResponse?.RequestMessage?.RequestUri?.Authority;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(upstream))
            {
                responseContext.HttpContext.Response.Headers["X-Handled-By"] = upstream;
            }
            return ValueTask.CompletedTask;
        });
    });

var app = builder.Build();

if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseSwaggerUI(options =>
    {
        options.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/sql/swagger.json", "MySurveyApp — SqlService API");
        options.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/nosql/swagger.json", "MySurveyApp — NoSqlService API");
        options.RoutePrefix = "swagger";
    });
}

app.MapReverseProxy();

app.Run();

FILE: src\MySurveyApp.NoSqlService\Program.cs

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
using Microsoft.OpenApi;
using MongoDB.Driver;
using MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.Configurations;
using MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.Models;
using MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.Services;
using System.Text;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

// Налаштування та реєстрація MongoDB
builder.Services.Configure<MongoDbSettings>(
    builder.Configuration.GetSection("MongoDbSettings"));

var mongoSettings = builder.Configuration.GetSection("MongoDbSettings").Get<MongoDbSettings>()
    ?? new MongoDbSettings
    {
        ConnectionString = "mongodb://mongoadmin:mongosecretpassword@localhost:27017/?authSource=admin",
        DatabaseName = "MySurveyAppAnalyticsDb",
        ResponsesCollectionName = "survey_responses"
    };

builder.Services.AddSingleton<IMongoClient>(_ => new MongoClient(mongoSettings.ConnectionString));
builder.Services.AddSingleton<IMongoDatabase>(sp =>
    sp.GetRequiredService<IMongoClient>().GetDatabase(mongoSettings.DatabaseName));
builder.Services.AddSingleton<IMongoCollection<SurveyResponseDocument>>(sp =>
    sp.GetRequiredService<IMongoDatabase>().GetCollection<SurveyResponseDocument>(mongoSettings.ResponsesCollectionName));

// HTTP-клієнт для запитів збагачення даних до SqlService
var sqlServiceUrl = builder.Configuration["SqlServiceSettings:BaseUrl"] ?? "http://localhost:5001";
var internalApiKey = builder.Configuration["InternalApiKey"] ?? "YOUR_SUPER_SECRET_MICROSERVICE_KEY_12345";

builder.Services.AddHttpClient<SqlEnrichmentClient>(client =>
{
    client.BaseAddress = new Uri(sqlServiceUrl);
    client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Add("X-Api-Key", internalApiKey);
    client.Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5);
});

// Автентифікація JWT (для розшифрування UserId із токена авторизованих користувачів)
builder.Services.AddAuthentication(JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme)
    .AddJwtBearer(options =>
    {
        var secretKey = "YOUR_SUPER_SECRET_KEY_FOR_JWT_SURVEY_APP_2026!";
        options.TokenValidationParameters = new TokenValidationParameters
        {
            ValidateIssuer = false,
            ValidateAudience = false,
            ValidateLifetime = true,
            ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,
            IssuerSigningKey = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(secretKey))
        };
    });

builder.Services.AddAuthorization();
builder.Services.AddControllers();

// Налаштування Swagger (OpenAPI 2.x)
builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();
builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen(options =>
{
    options.SwaggerDoc("v1", new OpenApiInfo
    {
        Title = "MySurveyApp NoSqlService API",
        Version = "v1"
    });

    options.AddSecurityDefinition("Bearer", new OpenApiSecurityScheme
    {
        Name = "Authorization",
        Type = SecuritySchemeType.Http,
        Scheme = "Bearer",
        BearerFormat = "JWT",
        In = ParameterLocation.Header,
        Description = "Вставте JWT-токен (без слова Bearer)"
    });

    options.AddSecurityDefinition("AnonymousToken", new OpenApiSecurityScheme
    {
        Name = "X-Anonymous-Token",
        Type = SecuritySchemeType.ApiKey,
        In = ParameterLocation.Header,
        Description = "Вставте UUID анонімного користувача"
    });

    options.AddSecurityRequirement(document => new OpenApiSecurityRequirement
    {
        [new OpenApiSecuritySchemeReference("Bearer", document)] = [],
        [new OpenApiSecuritySchemeReference("AnonymousToken", document)] = []
    });
});

var app = builder.Build();

// Swagger UI доступний у Docker на кореневому шляху "/"
app.UseSwagger();
app.UseSwaggerUI(c =>
{
    c.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/v1/swagger.json", "MySurveyApp NoSQL API v1");
    c.RoutePrefix = string.Empty;
});

// Автоматичне створення покриваючого індексу idx_fast_diagram_totals при запуску
try
{
    var collection = app.Services.GetRequiredService<IMongoCollection<SurveyResponseDocument>>();
    var indexKeys = Builders<SurveyResponseDocument>.IndexKeys
        .Ascending(d => d.SurveyId)
        .Ascending(d => d.Answers)
        .Ascending(d => d.SurveyType);

    var indexModel = new CreateIndexModel<SurveyResponseDocument>(
        indexKeys,
        new CreateIndexOptions { Name = "idx_fast_diagram_totals" });

    await collection.Indexes.CreateOneAsync(indexModel);
}
catch (Exception ex)
{
    app.Logger.LogWarning(ex, "Не вдалося створити індекс MongoDB під час старту.");
}

// Конвеєр обробки HTTP-запитів
app.UseAuthentication();
app.UseAuthorization();
app.MapControllers();

app.Run();

================================================================================
FILE: src\MySurveyApp.NoSqlService\Controllers\AnalyticsController.cs
================================================================================
using System.Globalization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using MongoDB.Bson;
using MongoDB.Driver;
using MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.DTO;
using MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.Models;

namespace MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.Controllers;

[Route("api/internal/surveys")]
[ApiController]
public class AnalyticsController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IMongoCollection<SurveyResponseDocument> _responsesCollection;

    public AnalyticsController(IMongoCollection<SurveyResponseDocument> responsesCollection)
    {
        _responsesCollection = responsesCollection;
    }

    [HttpGet("{surveyId:int}/diagram")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(SurveyDiagramResponseDto), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> GetSurveyDiagram(
        int surveyId,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        // 1. Агрегація на рівні MongoDB (використовує покриваючий індекс за survey_id та answers)
        var matchStage = new BsonDocument("$match", new BsonDocument("survey_id", surveyId));
        var groupStage = new BsonDocument("$group", new BsonDocument
        {
            { "_id", "$answers" },
            { "surveyType", new BsonDocument("$first", "$survey_type") },
            { "count", new BsonDocument("$sum", 1) }
        });

        var pipeline = PipelineDefinition<SurveyResponseDocument, BsonDocument>.Create(
            matchStage,
            groupStage);

        using var cursor = await _responsesCollection.AggregateAsync(pipeline, cancellationToken: cancellationToken);
        var rawGroups = await cursor.ToListAsync(cancellationToken);

        if (rawGroups.Count == 0)
        {
            return Ok(new SurveyDiagramResponseDto(
                SurveyId: surveyId,
                SurveyType: "Unknown",
                TotalResponses: 0,
                Items: new List<DiagramItemDto>()));
        }

        var surveyType = rawGroups[0]["surveyType"].AsString;
        var normalizedType = surveyType.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        var isMultipleChoice = normalizedType is "multiplechoice" or "multiple";
        var isNumeric = normalizedType is "numeric" or "number" or "numberinput" or "openanswer";

        // 2. Нормалізація груп (для множинного вибору ["Б", "А"] і ["А", "Б"] об'єднуються в одну комбінацію)
        var aggregatedBuckets = rawGroups
            .Select(doc =>
            {
                var options = doc["_id"].AsBsonArray
                    .Select(item => item.AsString)
                    .ToList();

                if (isMultipleChoice)
                {
                    options = options
                        .OrderBy(x => x, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                        .ToList();
                }

                var key = string.Join("\u001F", options);
                var count = doc["count"].ToInt64();
                return new { Key = key, Options = options, Count = count };
            })
            .GroupBy(x => x.Key)
            .Select(g => new
            {
                Options = g.First().Options,
                Count = g.Sum(x => x.Count)
            })
            .ToList();

        var totalResponses = aggregatedBuckets.Sum(b => b.Count);

        // 3. Формування елементів діаграми залежно від типу питання
        var items = aggregatedBuckets.Select(bucket =>
        {
            var percentage = totalResponses > 0
                ? Math.Round((double)bucket.Count / totalResponses * 100, 2)
                : 0;

            if (isMultipleChoice)
            {
                return new DiagramItemDto(
                    Label: string.Join(" + ", bucket.Options),
                    Count: bucket.Count);
            }

            var singleValue = bucket.Options.FirstOrDefault() ?? string.Empty;

            if (isNumeric)
            {
                double? parsedNumber = double.TryParse(
                    singleValue.Replace(',', '.'),
                    NumberStyles.Any,
                    CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                    out var num)
                    ? num
                    : null;

                return new DiagramItemDto(
                    Label: singleValue,
                    Count: bucket.Count);
            }

            // Один варіант відповіді (SingleChoice)
            return new DiagramItemDto(
                Label: singleValue,
                Count: bucket.Count);
        });

        var sortedItems = items.OrderByDescending(i => i.Count).ToList();

        return Ok(new SurveyDiagramResponseDto(
            SurveyId: surveyId,
            SurveyType: surveyType,
            TotalResponses: totalResponses,
            Items: sortedItems));
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src\MySurveyApp.NoSqlService\Controllers\ResponsesController.cs
================================================================================
using System.Security.Claims;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using MongoDB.Driver;
using MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.DTO;
using MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.Models;
using MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.Services;
using MySurveyApp.Shared.DTO;

namespace MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.Controllers;

[Route("api/internal/responses")]
[ApiController]
public class ResponsesController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IMongoCollection<SurveyResponseDocument> _responsesCollection;
    private readonly SqlEnrichmentClient _sqlClient;

    public ResponsesController(
        IMongoCollection<SurveyResponseDocument> responsesCollection,
        SqlEnrichmentClient sqlClient)
    {
        _responsesCollection = responsesCollection;
        _sqlClient = sqlClient;
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status502BadGateway)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> SubmitResponse(
        [FromBody] SubmitSurveyResponseDto dto,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        // 1. Витягуємо UserId з JWT-токена (якщо передано Authorization: Bearer ...)
        var userIdClaim = User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier);
        Guid? userId = Guid.TryParse(userIdClaim, out var parsedGuid) ? parsedGuid : null;

        // 2. Або беремо токен анонімного користувача із заголовка
        var anonymousToken = Request.Headers["X-Anonymous-Token"].FirstOrDefault();

        if (userId == null && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(anonymousToken))
        {
            return Unauthorized(new
            {
                message = "Необхідно передати JWT-токен (Authorization: Bearer) або токен аноніма (X-Anonymous-Token)."
            });
        }

        if (dto.SelectedOptionIds.Count == 0 && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dto.OpenAnswer))
        {
            return BadRequest(new { message = "Необхідно передати хоча б один ID варіанту або відкриту відповідь." });
        }

        // 3. Отримуємо збагачений контекст із SqlService
        EnrichmentContextResponseDto? context;
        try
        {
            context = await _sqlClient.FetchContextAsync(
                userId,
                anonymousToken,
                dto.SurveyId,
                dto.SelectedOptionIds,
                cancellationToken);
        }
        catch (HttpRequestException ex)
        {
            return StatusCode(StatusCodes.Status502BadGateway, new
            {
                message = "Помилка отримання даних із SQL-сервісу.",
                details = ex.Message
            });
        }

        if (context == null)
        {
            return BadRequest(new { message = "Не вдалося сформувати контекст відповіді." });
        }

        // 4. Формуємо знеособлений документ для MongoDB (жодних ID чи токенів користувача)
        var finalAnswers = context.ResolvedAnswers.Count > 0
            ? context.ResolvedAnswers
            : new List<string> { dto.OpenAnswer!.Trim() };

        var document = new SurveyResponseDocument
        {
            SurveyId = context.SurveyId,
            SurveyType = context.SurveyType,
            Answers = finalAnswers,
            Region = context.SurveyRegion,
            SurveyDomain = context.SurveyDomain,
            SurveyTopic = context.SurveyTopic,
            AnsweredAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
            IsPreferredDomain = dto.IsPreferredDomain,
            UserSnapshot = new UserSnapshot
            {
                Gender = context.UserSnapshot.Gender,
                BirthYear = context.UserSnapshot.BirthYear.HasValue ? new DateTime(context.UserSnapshot.BirthYear.Value, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc) : null,
                RegionName = context.UserSnapshot.RegionName,
                OccDomainName = context.UserSnapshot.OccDomainName,
                OccupationName = context.UserSnapshot.OccupationName,
                HobbyName = context.UserSnapshot.HobbyName
            }
        };

        // 5. Асинхронний неблокуючий запис у MongoDB
        await _responsesCollection.InsertOneAsync(document, cancellationToken: cancellationToken);

        return Created(string.Empty, new
        {
            message = "Відповідь успішно зафіксована.",
            responseId = document.Id
        });
    }
}

FILE: src\MySurveyApp.NoSqlService\DTO\SubmitSurveyResponseDto.cs

namespace MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.DTO;

public record SubmitSurveyResponseDto(
    int SurveyId,
    List<int> SelectedOptionIds,
    string? OpenAnswer = null,
    bool IsPreferredDomain = true
);

public record SurveyDiagramResponseDto(
    int SurveyId,
    string SurveyType,
    long TotalResponses,
    List<DiagramItemDto> Items
);

public record DiagramItemDto(
    string Label,
    long Count
);

================================================================================
FILE: src\MySurveyApp.NoSqlService\Models\SurveyResponseDocument.cs
================================================================================
using MongoDB.Bson;
using MongoDB.Bson.Serialization.Attributes;

namespace MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.Models;

public class SurveyResponseDocument
{
    [BsonId]
    [BsonRepresentation(BsonType.ObjectId)]
    public string? Id { get; set; }

    [BsonElement("survey_id")]
    public int SurveyId { get; set; }

    [BsonElement("survey_type")]
    public string SurveyType { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [BsonElement("answers")]
    public List<string> Answers { get; set; } = new();

    [BsonElement("region")]
    public string Region { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [BsonElement("survey_domain")]
    public string SurveyDomain { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [BsonElement("survey_topic")]
    public string SurveyTopic { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [BsonElement("answered_at")]
    public DateTime AnsweredAt { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow;

    [BsonElement("is_preferred_domain")]
    public bool IsPreferredDomain { get; set; }

    [BsonElement("user_snapshot")]
    public UserSnapshot UserSnapshot { get; set; } = new();
}

FILE: src\MySurveyApp.NoSqlService\Services\SqlEnrichmentClient.cs
================================================================================
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using MySurveyApp.Shared.DTO;

namespace MySurveyApp.NoSqlService.Services;

public class SqlEnrichmentClient
{
    private readonly HttpClient _httpClient;

    public SqlEnrichmentClient(HttpClient httpClient)
    {
        _httpClient = httpClient;
    }

    public async Task<EnrichmentContextResponseDto?> FetchContextAsync(
        Guid? userId,
        string? anonymousToken,
        int surveyId,
        List<int> selectedOptionIds,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var requestPayload = new EnrichmentContextRequestDto(
            userId,
            anonymousToken,
            surveyId,
            selectedOptionIds);

        var response = await _httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync(
            "/api/internal/enrichment-context",
            requestPayload,
            cancellationToken);

        if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
        {
            var errorBody = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(cancellationToken);
            throw new HttpRequestException(
                $"SqlService повернув помилку {(int)response.StatusCode}: {errorBody}");
        }

        return await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<EnrichmentContextResponseDto>(
            cancellationToken: cancellationToken);
    }

    public async Task<bool> RecordInteractionAsync(
    int surveyId,
    string userId,
    CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        try
        {
            var payload = new
            {
                surveyId = surveyId,
                userId = userId
            };

            var response = await _httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync(
                "/api/Surveys/interact",
                payload,
                cancellationToken);

            return response.IsSuccessStatusCode;
        }
        catch
        {
            return false;
        }
    }
}

public record EnrichmentContextRequestDto(
    Guid? UserId,
    string? AnonymousToken,

    List<int> SelectedOptionIds

    int SurveyId,
    string SurveyType,
    List<string> ResolvedAnswers,
    string SurveyRegion,
    string SurveyDomain,
    string SurveyTopic,
    UserSnapshotDto UserSnapshot
);

FROM mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/sdk:10.0 AS build
WORKDIR /app

# Копіюємо файли проектів для кешування відновлення пакетів
COPY MySurveyApp.SqlService/MySurveyApp.SqlService.csproj MySurveyApp.SqlService/
COPY MySurveyApp.Shared/MySurveyApp.Shared.csproj MySurveyApp.Shared/

# Відновлюємо залежності
RUN dotnet restore MySurveyApp.SqlService/MySurveyApp.SqlService.csproj

# Копіюємо всі вихідні файли обох проектів
COPY MySurveyApp.SqlService/ MySurveyApp.SqlService/
COPY MySurveyApp.Shared/ MySurveyApp.Shared/

# Публікуємо сервіс
WORKDIR /app/MySurveyApp.SqlService
RUN dotnet publish -c Release -o /app/publish /p:UseAppHost=false

# 2. Етап запуску (Runtime)
FROM mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/aspnet:10.0 AS final
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=build /app/publish .

EXPOSE 8080
ENV ASPNETCORE_HTTP_PORTS=8080

ENTRYPOINT ["dotnet", "MySurveyApp.SqlService.dll"]

FILE: src\MySurveyApp.SqlService\Program.cs

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
using MySurveyApp.SqlService.Data;
using MySurveyApp.SqlService.Models;
using Swashbuckle.AspNetCore.SwaggerGen;
using Swashbuckle.AspNetCore.SwaggerUI;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

// Отримуємо рядок підключення з appsettings.json
var connectionString = builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("Postgres");

// Реєструємо SurveyDbContext у DI-контейнері
builder.Services.AddDbContext<SurveyDbContext>(options =>
    options.UseNpgsql(connectionString));

// Додаємо підтримку контролерів 
builder.Services.AddControllers();

// Реєструємо Swagger / OpenAPI
builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();
builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen();

builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen(options =>
{
    var xmlFilename = $"{Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetName().Name}.xml";
    var xmlPath = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, xmlFilename);
    if (File.Exists(xmlPath))
    {
        options.IncludeXmlComments(xmlPath);
    }
});

builder.Services.AddAuthentication(options =>
{
    options.DefaultAuthenticateScheme = JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme;
    options.DefaultChallengeScheme = JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme;
    options.DefaultForbidScheme = JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme;
})
.AddJwtBearer(options =>
{
    // Замініть цей рядок на ваш секретний ключ (має бути мінімум 32 символи)
    var secretKey = "YOUR_SUPER_SECRET_KEY_FOR_JWT_SURVEY_APP_2026!";

    options.TokenValidationParameters = new TokenValidationParameters
    {
        ValidateIssuer = false, // Для спрощення розробки вимикаємо
        ValidateAudience = false, // Для спрощення розробки вимикаємо
        ValidateLifetime = true, // Перевіряти час життя токена (Expires)
        ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,
        IssuerSigningKey = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(secretKey))
    };
});

var app = builder.Build();

using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
{
    var dbContext = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<SurveyDbContext>();

    dbContext.Database.Migrate();


    await dbContext.Database.ExecuteSqlRawAsync(Triggers.CreateTriggersSql);
}

//робимо доступним у Docker
app.UseSwagger();
app.UseSwaggerUI(c =>
{
    c.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/v1/swagger.json", "MySurveyApp SQL API v1");
    c.RoutePrefix = string.Empty; // Відкриває UI на кореневому шляху "/"
});



// Налаштовуємо маршрутизацію запитів
app.UseRouting();

app.UseAuthentication(); 
app.UseAuthorization();

app.MapControllers();

app.Run();
